HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000447/B001","root_001033/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2a21036257f85f44e40d","text":"1. **No fear of aftermath because authority owns the consequence.** Lexical evidence: خ و ف B001 \"fearful anticipation of harm\"; ع ق ب B006 \"the end and outcome\"; ر ب ب B001 \"lordship, ownership, and authority\" in 91:14. Structural cue: the line follows the Lord's damdama/sawa. Assigned role: خوف would be anticipation of retaliatory harm; عقبى is the result or aftermath of the act. Abductive move: I infer that the subject has no fear because the action belongs to sovereign authority, not exposed wrongdoing; the packet supplies Lordship and aftermath. Reading change: the ayah closes as judicial fearlessness, not heedless indifference.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000447/B001","root_001033/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_35670385d42c700d3927","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:15:2\",\"root_000447\",[\"B001\"]],[\"91:15:3\",\"root_001033\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sonuçtan korkmama, önceki ayetteki Rablik yetkisi sayesinde pervasızlık değil, hükmün meşru sahibine ait yargısal korkusuzluktur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001033/B011"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4712200d13b6e6b85371","text":"3. **No residue or posterity capable of answering back.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ع ق ب/B011` is residue or remaining trace and `ع ق ب/B004` is posterity; 91:14 activates `ع ق ر/B004`, stopped generation, and `د م د م/B001`, eradication. **Structural cue and role:** barrenness plus eradication leaves the aftermath without a surviving line or remainder, so the dormant branches assign absence to what follows. **Abductive move:** this is exploratory because `عقبى` most directly fits outcome; the no-posterity reading arises from the exact neighboring eradication and generation branches. **Reading change:** the ending can also register how comprehensively the event closes futurity.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000447/B004","root_001033/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4d52f64a2da8d990ac65","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:15:2\",\"root_000447\",[\"B004\"]],[\"91:15:3\",\"root_001033\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Yıkım topluluğu azaltır; buna karşılık hükmü veren Allah sonuç korkusuyla hiçbir eksilmeye uğramaz.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001033/B008","root_001272/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_63d2e81869767ecb8732","text":"2. **No claimant can follow up or reverse the judgment.** Lexical evidence: ع ق ب B008 \"following up and challenging\"; ق و ل B010 \"arrogating judgment over someone\" in 91:13; ك ذ ب B002 denial; ر ب ب B001 authority. Structural cue: the denial/challenge of the messenger is followed by a judgment that no one can challenge. Assigned role: عقبها is the possible review/appeal after a ruling. Abductive move: I infer a legal scene where a punished party might seek reversal, but the divine act admits no counter-claim; the packet supplies challenge/review and authority branches. Reading change: the ayah becomes a statement of non-appealability: no \"معقب\" can overturn the result.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000186/B001","root_000521/B002","root_000521/B006","root_001033/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6794017411e6d961265e","text":"3. **Outcome as the rear trace of the people's own act.** Lexical evidence: ع ق ب B002 \"heel and rear trace\"; B005 \"coming after in succession\"; ذ ن ب B002 \"tail or rear end\" and B006 \"share\" in 91:14; ت ل و B001/B003 following/remainder in 91:2. Structural cue: the packet begins with orderly following and ends with aftermath. Assigned role: عقبى is the step immediately behind the action, the trace they made. Abductive move: I infer that the punishment follows in the track of their own deed; the packet supplies follow/remainder/tail/after. Reading change: the final line reads as moral kinematics: consequence treads the heel-print of action.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_722d783673847253da39","text":"No later ayah follows within the packet, but rereading the whole sequence made the final line gather the entire following-chain: تلا, ذنب, and عقب all speak of what comes after. The ending is therefore not an appended threat; it is the last disclosure of the ordered succession already modeled by moon after sun, deed after denial, and consequence after sin.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001033/B012"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_74b1e9c5c0abd7c743f2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:15:3\",\"root_001033\",[\"B012\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Sonuç, azgınlığın suçlulara bıraktığı sarp geçit ve çıkıntılı kaya gibi aşılması güç bir araziye dönüşebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000447/B004","root_000486/B001","root_001033/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8c3f1a9097a7241d9f1a","text":"5. **Fear as diminished/taken-away state refused to the divine actor.** Lexical evidence: خ و ف B004 \"diminishing by taking away\"; د م د م B001 complete eradication; ع ق ب B007 punitive consequence. Structural cue: the negated fear follows a total taking-away of the people. Assigned role: fear's \"diminishing\" branch is activated only by contrast: the Lord's act diminishes them, not Himself. Abductive move: I infer a reversal of vulnerability: those who overstepped are reduced; the punisher is not reduced by anticipating consequence. Reading change: لا يخاف عقبها becomes a compact statement that divine punitive action carries no vulnerability or depletion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001033/B004","root_001033/B011"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9a5978e5eb155351a017","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:15:3\",\"root_001033\",[\"B004\",\"B011\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Yok ediliş, karşılık verebilecek bir devam ya da soy bırakmayan bir sonuç olarak okunabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000447/B001","root_001033/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a644b63821ecfbb128cb","text":"2. **Judgment with no successful reviewer or reverser.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ع ق ب/B008` is following up a judgment to question, challenge, or reverse it; fixed `خ و ف/B001` supplies apprehension. **Structural cue and role:** the nearest expressed agent is `ربهم` in 91:14, followed by completed eradication and equalization; the review branch gives `عقباها` the institutional role of a possible challenge after judgment. **Abductive move:** carrying the prior subject forward is a syntactic inference, and reading absence of fear as absence of a reversing claimant is branch-driven. **Reading change:** the clause can mark final, unreviewable execution rather than only an emotional state.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000808/B004","root_000937/B005","root_001033/B012"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b79c32788853d3a16d70","text":"4. **Aftermath as steep pass or hard residue.** Lexical evidence: ع ق ب B012 \"steep pass and jutting rock\"; B011 \"residue and remaining trace\"; ط غ ي B005 high outcrop in 91:11; ش ق و B004 mountain flank in 91:12. Structural cue: terrain branches recur across transgression, wretchedness, and consequence. Assigned role: عقبها becomes the hard terrain after the event: the pass, ledge, or residue left behind. Abductive move: I infer that moral aftermath has a topography that must be traversed by the guilty but does not threaten the judging Lord. Reading change: \"her aftermath\" is not abstract time after punishment only; it is the difficult remaining terrain created by rebellion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001033/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c0fb8d216931be95fbf7","text":"4. **Aftermath as scheduled successor.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ع ق ب/B005` is one thing following another in succession. **Structural cue and role:** 91:14's tightly chained *fa-* verbs make each act follow the last, and this final coordinated clause names the successor to the whole chain. **Abductive move:** formal succession is explicit; treating aftermath as structurally scheduled rather than accidental is inferred. **Reading change:** `عقباها` becomes the necessary next position generated by the preceding sequence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000521/B001","root_001033/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d29bf274d822541c4742","text":"1. **Consequence as the act's inevitable rear result.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ع ق ب/B006` is the end or aftermath an act brings, and fixed `خ و ف/B001` is anticipation of harm from a sign; 91:14 `ذ ن ب/B001` already defines offense through culpable consequence. **Structural cue and role:** the final clause follows the entire denial-to-leveling chain and names what comes after it, making `عقباها` the rear result toward which prior actions point. **Abductive move:** sequence and outcome vocabulary are supplied; treating the whole narrative as the sign from which an aftermath could be anticipated is inferred. **Reading change:** the close converts the preceding event into a causal history whose meaning lies in its end.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ec90da0954f871d3f400","text":"No later ayah exists in the clipped focus window, so no later surprise emerged.
